## C-916 P1 — parry slow-motion PIE witness

`Scripts/Editor/pie_legend.py` group `l8` uses `URPGPlayerParry::Press()` followed by an actual A4 blade contact, which passes through `RPGDamageResolver::Resolve()` and its `NotifyParried()` call. It records onset, 0.30 scale, real duration, restoration, and a real-contact `rpg.Legend 0` control. After Lead's H9 rejection, L8 now runs before the kill scenario and refuses cleanly if the boss is absent before or after reset; offline driver checks pass 3/3. Driver/run details and the two-route Lead commands are in `Documentation/Boss/P1_LEGEND_PARRY_RUN.md`. Neither live route has been run by Codex.

Acceptance limitation: current player parry cooldown is 0.70 s, longer than the 0.60 s slow-motion request cooldown, so a second accepted perfect parry inside that policy cooldown is unreachable via the player API. L8 verifies a refused second press/no new scope and labels this gap; it does not claim the requested second-perfect-contact assertion. Lead review or a revised, approved test seam is needed for that criterion.

## G044 selected-definition provenance checks (Queue L, 2026-11-04)

Before saving definitions, the Editor authoring script must reject a missing,
mismatched, stale, or malformed source snapshot and compare every reflected
property readback to its authored typed value. The offline manifest validator
must require the carried source snapshot SHA-256 and verify the canonical
manifest digest. The read-only selection validator then checks selected object
paths against receipt package paths, definition snapshots, phase, and digest.
These are acceptance requirements; no G044 assets/snapshot currently exist, so
the Editor actions and receipt checks remain unrun pending Claude's fresh
readback.

BUILD-115: Vertical separation on floors (BOSS-AWR-005; C-577, 2026-11-01): Bug fix in ARPGFrameworkBossCharacter::FloorSeparationCm() in Source/OneBossFight/Boss/RPGFrameworkBossCharacter.cpp/.h. The earlier snapshot implementation used |Target.Z - Boss.Z| between actor origins; because the boss capsule half-height is 165 cm and the player capsule half-height is 98 cm, on flat ground their origins were already 67 cm apart, causing any card with MaxVerticalDeltaCm <= 50 to be falsely refused on flat ground. Fixed by computing feet-to-feet distance using GetSimpleCollisionCylinder of both actors. Build exit 0 (B_AWR005/build2_raw.log sha256 3bb0769b971873371f965862200a959bc8b32872b6e838db9970c310fe38f73a); full suite 221/221 PASS, exit 0 (B_AWR005/full2_raw.log sha256 1569002f1c14b3f10f6a507b44f086a7110cf190baead9bf93674cca3465a159, DLL c20291e2c02fecfe). Content: DA_Sevarog_VerticalProof_AWR005 + L_SevarogFrameworkProofAWR005 (cards: VerticalProof with MaxVerticalDeltaCm=50, AntiGreed_F5; ledge cube top Z=110 cm). Live PIE proof on L_SevarogFrameworkProofAWR005 (B_AWR005/pie_ledge/editor_raw.log sha256 3ccc175b17dadd8f38541d627318efb5f14dc44ef606ccf7c53120ba1cf9039a with driver -Shift 0.3:0,50,115): line 1576 proves candidate eligible on flat ground (score=0.784425 seq=1, verifying fix and control); line 1648 player lifted onto ledge; line 1657 proves same card refused (refusal reason=MoveModel.Vertical seq=2, delta Z = 115 cm > 50 cm); line 1733 RESULT CompletedWithDamage acceptedHits=2 attacks=2. The latency sheet prescribes six isolated Lead sessions (legacy/data × 30/60/120 FPS), three action observations per session, route/cadence verification, and retained logs/hashes. `pie_input_montage_latency.py` does not set FPS and is not registered in `RunDrivers.ps1`, so it is invoked directly with `RunEditorScript.ps1`; `observed_ms` is injection-to-first-tick-observed montage, not device or pixel latency. The hit-feel table separates source-configured effects from absent/unverified hit-specific effects and Blueprint overrides. The telegraph table separates Freeze targets and tracker configuration from actual response windows; Lead captures remain required. KI-190 options A/B/C are presented without selection or tuning. See `Documentation/Boss/W_C036_C039_FEEL_MEASUREMENT_SHEETS.md`. Documentation only; no Editor/build/PIE run.
